        "import torch, torch.nn as nn, torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch_geometric.data import Data, DataLoader\n",
        "from torch_geometric.nn import GCNConv, global_mean_pool\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from tqdm import tqdm\n",
        "import os\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "print(\"Using:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# --- Change this path to where you uploaded your dataset files ---\n",
        "DATA_DIR = \"/content\"\n",
        "dataset_name = \"PenDigits\"\n",
        "\n",
        "train_path = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, f\"{dataset_name}_TRAIN.ts\")\n",
        "test_path = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, f\"{dataset_name}_TEST.ts\")\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train, y_train = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(train_path)\n",
        "X_test, y_test = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(test_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Label encode\n",
        "classes = sorted(set(y_train))\n",
        "label2id = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(classes)}\n",
        "y_train = np.array([label2id[y] for y in y_train])\n",
        "y_test = np.array([label2id[y] for y in y_test])\n",
        "\n",
        "def to_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "    n, c = X_df.shape\n",
        "    t = len(X_df.iloc[0, 0])\n",
        "    out = np.zeros((n, t, c))\n",
        "    for i in range(n):\n",
        "        for j in range(c):\n",
        "            out[i, :, j] = X_df.iloc[i, j]\n",
        "    return out\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train = to_numpy(X_train)\n",
        "X_test = to_numpy(X_test)\n",
        "print(\"Shapes:\", X_train.shape, X_test.shape)  # (samples, timesteps, channels)\n"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "eu33zRnQxcIU",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "def build_chain_graph(x):\n",
        "    T = x.shape[0]\n",
        "    src = torch.arange(0, T - 1, dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "    dst = torch.arange(1, T, dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "    edge_index = torch.stack([torch.cat([src, dst]), torch.cat([dst, src])], dim=0)\n",
        "    return edge_index\n",
        "\n",
        "class PenDigitsGraphDataset(torch.utils.data.Dataset):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, X, y):\n",
        "        self.X, self.y = X, y\n",
        "    def __len__(self): return len(self.X)\n",
        "    def __getitem__(self, i):\n",
        "        x = torch.tensor(self.X[i], dtype=torch.float)\n",
        "        edge_index = build_chain_graph(x)\n",
        "        return Data(x=x, edge_index=edge_index, y=torch.tensor(self.y[i], dtype=torch.long))\n",
        "\n",
        "train_ds = PenDigitsGraphDataset(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "test_ds = PenDigitsGraphDataset(X_test, y_test)\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=64, shuffle=True)\n",
        "test_loader = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=64)\n"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/"

            "  test_loader = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=64)\n"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "class TemporalBlock(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_ch, out_ch, k=3, d=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.conv = nn.Conv1d(in_ch, out_ch, kernel_size=k, padding=(k - 1) * d, dilation=d)\n",
        "        self.bn = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_ch)\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.bn(self.conv(x)))\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN_GCN_GoG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_ch, tcn_hidden=32, gcn_hidden=64, num_classes=10):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        # --- Fix: Match input size to dataset channel count ---\n",
        "        self.input_proj = nn.Linear(in_ch, tcn_hidden)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Temporal ConvNet\n",
        "        self.tcn = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            TemporalBlock(tcn_hidden, tcn_hidden),\n",
        "            TemporalBlock(tcn_hidden, tcn_hidden)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Graph ConvNet\n",
        "        self.gcn1 = GCNConv(tcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gcn2 = GCNConv(gcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Classifier (Graph-of-Graphs)\n",
        "        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(gcn_hidden, 128)\n",
        "        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, num_classes)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, data):\n",
        "        x, edge_index, batch = data.x, data.edge_index, data.batch\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Input projection\n",
        "        x = self.input_proj(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Temporal conv (apply on each sample’s node features)\n",
        "        # To use Conv1d, we need [B, C, T], but each graph is separate, so skip here.\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Graph convolution layers\n",
        "        x = F.relu(self.gcn1(x, edge_index))\n",
        "        x = F.relu(self.gcn2(x, edge_index))\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Pool each graph into one vector\n",
        "        x = global_mean_pool(x, batch)\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = F.relu(self.fc1(x))\n",
        "        return self.fc2(x)\n"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "O0St4b7gMjDJ"
      },
      "execution_count": null,
      "outputs": []
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "model = TCN_GCN_GoG(in_ch=X_train.shape[2], num_classes=len(classes)).to(device)\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=1e-3)\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "\n",
        "def train_epoch():\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    total_loss = 0\n",
        "    for data in tqdm(train_loader):\n",
        "        data = data.to(device)\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        out = model(data)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(out, data.y)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "        total_loss += loss.item() * data.num_graphs\n",
        "    return total_loss / len(train_loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def evaluate(loader):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    correct = 0\n",
        "    for data in loader:\n",
        "        data = data.to(device)\n",
        "        out = model(data)\n",
        "        pred = out.argmax(dim=1)\n",
        "        correct += (pred == data.y).sum().item()\n",
        "    return correct / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, 50):\n",
        "    loss = train_epoch()\n",
        "    acc = evaluate(test_loader)\n",
        "    print(f\"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Loss: {loss:.4f} | Test Acc: {acc*100:.2f}%\")\n"
      ],

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# =============================================================\n",
        "# PenDigits GoG Model (TCN → GCN → GoG → Classifier)\n",
        "# =============================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset\n",
        "from tqdm import tqdm\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "print(\"Device:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Load the PenDigits dataset from .ts files\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe\n",
        "\n",
        "def load_pendigits(train_path, test_path):\n",
        "    X_train_df, y_train = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(train_path)\n",
        "    X_test_df, y_test = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(test_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Convert each sample (which can be Series or ndarray) to numpy arrays\n",
        "    def to_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "        all_series = []\n",
        "        for row in X_df.itertuples(index=False):\n",
        "            seqs = []\n",
        "            for v in row:\n",
        "                if hasattr(v, \"to_numpy\"):\n",
        "                    seqs.append(v.to_numpy())\n",
        "                else:\n",
        "                    seqs.append(np.array(v))\n",
        "            all_series.append(np.stack(seqs))\n",
        "        return np.stack(all_series)\n",
        "\n",
        "    X_train = to_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "    X_test = to_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "    y_train = np.array(y_train).astype(int)\n",
        "    y_test = np.array(y_test).astype(int)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# 🟢 Provide correct paths to your dataset files in Google Colab\n",
        "X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test = load_pendigits(\n",
        "    \"/content/PenDigits_TRAIN.ts\",\n",
        "    \"/content/PenDigits_TEST.ts\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"X_train shape: {X_train.shape}, X_test shape: {X_test.shape}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Convert to PyTorch tensors\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "train_ds = TensorDataset(torch.tensor(X_train, dtype=torch.float32),\n",
        "                         torch.tensor(y_train, dtype=torch.long))\n",
        "test_ds = TensorDataset(torch.tensor(X_test, dtype=torch.float32),\n",
        "                        torch.tensor(y_test, dtype=torch.long))\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=128, shuffle=True)\n",
        "test_loader = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False)\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Temporal Convolutional Network block\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "class TCNBlock(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_c, out_c, k=3, dilation=1):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.net = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Conv1d(in_c, out_c, k, padding=\"same\", dilation=dilation),\n",
        "            nn.BatchNorm1d(out_c),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "            nn.Conv1d(out_c, out_c, k, padding=\"same\", dilation=dilation),\n",
        "            nn.BatchNorm1d(out_c),\n",
        "            nn.ReLU()\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        self.res = nn.Conv1d(in_c, out_c, 1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: (B, F, T)\n",
        "        return self.net(x) + self.res(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Graph Convolution Layer\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_f, out_f):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_f, out_f)\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, adj):\n",
        "        deg = torch.sum(adj, dim=-1, keepdim=True) + 1e-6\n",
        "        norm_adj = adj / deg\n",
        "        out = torch.bmm(norm_adj, x)\n",
        "        out = self.linear(out)\n",
        "        return F.relu(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Build adjacency: combine temporal + cosine similarity\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "def build_adjacency(x):\n",
        "    # x: (B, N, F)\n",
        "    B, N, F = x.size()\n",
        "    cos = F.normalize(x, dim=-1)\n",
        "    adj = torch.bmm(cos, cos.transpose(1,2))\n",
        "    temporal = torch.eye(N, device=x.device).unsqueeze(0).repeat(B,1,1)\n",
        "    temporal = temporal.roll(1,1) + temporal.roll(-1,1)\n",
        "    adj = 0.5*adj + 0.5*temporal\n",
        "    adj = torch.clamp(adj, 0, 1)\n",
        "    return adj\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Full TCN → GCN → GoG Model\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "class GoGModel(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, input_dim=2, hidden_dim=64, graph_dim=64, num_classes=10):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.tcn = TCNBlock(input_dim, hidden_dim)\n",
        "        self.gcn1 = GraphConv(hidden_dim, graph_dim)\n",
        "        self.gcn2 = GraphConv(graph_dim, graph_dim)\n",
        "        self.meta_fc1 = nn.Linear(graph_dim, 128)\n",
        "        self.meta_fc2 = nn.Linear(128, num_classes)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # TCN expects (B, F, T)\n",
        "        x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)\n",
        "        h = self.tcn(x)           # (B, hidden_dim, T)\n",
        "        h = h.permute(0, 2, 1)    # (B, T, hidden_dim)\n",
        "\n",
        "        adj = build_adjacency(h)\n",
        "        h = self.gcn1(h, adj)\n",
        "        h = self.gcn2(h, adj)\n",
        "        g_repr = h.mean(dim=1)    # (B, graph_dim)\n",
        "\n",
        "        meta_h = F.relu(self.meta_fc1(g_repr))\n",
        "        return self.meta_fc2(meta_h)\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Training setup\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "model = GoGModel().to(device)\n",
        "opt = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=1e-4, weight_decay=1e-4)\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "\n",
        "def train_epoch():\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    total_loss = 0\n",
        "    for xb, yb in tqdm(train_loader, leave=False, desc=\"Train\"):\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        opt.zero_grad()\n",
        "        pred = model(xb)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(pred, yb)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        opt.step()\n",
        "        total_loss += loss.item() * xb.size(0)\n",
        "    return total_loss / len(train_loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "def evaluate(loader):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    correct = 0\n",
        "    with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "        for xb, yb in loader:\n",
        "            xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "            pred = model(xb).argmax(1)\n",
        "            correct += (pred == yb).sum().item()\n",
        "    return correct / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "# Train\n",
        "# -------------------------------------------------------------\n",
        "epochs = 50\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, epochs+1):\n",
        "    loss = train_epoch()\n",
        "    acc = evaluate(test_loader)\n",
        "    print(f\"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Loss {loss:.4f} | Test Acc {acc*100:.2f}%\")\n"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "colab": {
          "base_uri": "https://localhost:8080/",
          "height": 391
        },
        "id": "Uv6zEXzZPWlX",
        "outputId": "32c9dc22-7155-44aa-f613-87427c4d6b04"
      },
      "execution_count": null,

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "Fixed TCN → GCN → GoG → Classifier for PenDigits dataset\n",
        "with correct Conv1d channel handling and batch normalization\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset\n",
        "from tqdm import tqdm\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "\n",
        "# -----------------------\n",
        "# 1️⃣  Define model parts\n",
        "# -----------------------\n",
        "\n",
        "class TemporalConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Simple temporal convolution block\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size=3):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.conv = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size, padding=1)\n",
        "        self.bn = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: (B, F, T)\n",
        "        x = self.conv(x)\n",
        "        x = self.bn(x)\n",
        "        return F.relu(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Basic GCN layer\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_features, out_features):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_features, out_features)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, adj):\n",
        "        # x: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        A_norm = adj + torch.eye(adj.size(-1), device=x.device)\n",
        "        D_inv_sqrt = torch.pow(A_norm.sum(dim=-1), -0.5)\n",
        "        D_inv_sqrt[torch.isinf(D_inv_sqrt)] = 0\n",
        "        D_mat = torch.diag_embed(D_inv_sqrt)\n",
        "        A_norm = torch.bmm(torch.bmm(D_mat, A_norm), D_mat)\n",
        "        out = torch.bmm(A_norm, x)\n",
        "        out = self.linear(out)\n",
        "        return F.relu(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GoG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Graph of Graphs aggregator\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_features, out_features):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Linear(in_features, out_features)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, graphs):\n",
        "        # graphs: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        # Mean pooling across graph nodes\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.fc(graphs.mean(dim=1)))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN_GCN_GoG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, num_features=2, num_classes=10):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.tcn = TemporalConv(num_features, 64)\n",
        "        self.gcn = GraphConv(64, 64)\n",
        "        self.gog = GoG(64, 128)\n",
        "        self.classifier = nn.Linear(128, num_classes)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)     # (B, F, T) for Conv1d\n",
        "        x = self.tcn(x)            # (B, 64, T)\n",
        "        x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)     # (B, T, 64)\n",
        "\n",
        "        B, N, F_dim = x.shape\n",
        "        # Simple adjacency: fully connected graph\n",
        "        adj = torch.ones(B, N, N, device=x.device)\n",
        "        x = self.gcn(x, adj)\n",
        "        x = self.gog(x)\n",
        "        return self.classifier(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "# -----------------------\n",
        "# 2️⃣  Load dataset (PenDigits)\n",
        "# -----------------------\n",
        "\n",
        "# Assuming you already have np arrays X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test\n",
        "# Each sample: shape (8, 2)\n",
        "# Replace with your actual data loading\n",
        "# Example dummy data for structure:\n",
        "\n",
        "# X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test = your_loaded_arrays_here\n",
        "\n",
        "# -----------------------\n",
        "# 2️⃣  Load PenDigits dataset from .ts files\n",
        "# -----------------------\n",
        "\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "\n",
        "# Adjust these paths if you uploaded the files somewhere else\n",
        "train_path = \"/content/PenDigits_TRAIN.ts\"\n",
        "test_path  = \"/content/PenDigits_TEST.ts\"\n",
        "\n",
        "# Load as pandas DataFrames\n",
        "X_train_df, y_train = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(train_path)\n",
        "X_test_df, y_test   = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(test_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Convert sktime format -> 3D numpy array (samples, timesteps, features)\n",
        "def to_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "    X_np = np.stack([np.stack([np.asarray(ts) for ts in row], axis=1) for row in X_df.itertuples(index=False)], axis=0)\n",
        "    return X_np\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train = to_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "X_test = to_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Loaded shapes:\", X_train.shape, X_test.shape)\n",
        "\n",
        "# Convert to tensors\n",
        "X_train = torch.tensor(X_train, dtype=torch.float32)\n",
        "y_train = torch.tensor(y_train.astype(int), dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "X_test  = torch.tensor(X_test, dtype=torch.float32)\n",
        "y_test  = torch.tensor(y_test.astype(int), dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=128, shuffle=True)\n",
        "test_loader = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=128)\n",
        "\n",
        "# -----------------------\n",
        "# 3️⃣  Train + Evaluate\n",
        "# -----------------------\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "model = TCN_GCN_GoG(num_features=2, num_classes=len(torch.unique(y_train))).to(device)\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=0.001)\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "\n",
        "def train_one_epoch():\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    total_loss = 0\n",
        "    for x, y in tqdm(train_loader, desc=\"Train\"):\n",
        "        x, y = x.to(device), y.to(device)\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        out = model(x)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(out, y)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "        total_loss += loss.item() * x.size(0)\n",
        "    return total_loss / len(train_loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "def evaluate():\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    correct = 0\n",
        "    with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "        for x, y in test_loader:\n",
        "            x, y = x.to(device), y.to(device)\n",
        "            preds = model(x).argmax(dim=1)\n",
        "            correct += (preds == y).sum().item()\n",
        "    return correct / len(test_loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "# -----------------------\n",
        "# 4️⃣  Run training\n",
        "# -----------------------\n",
        "\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, 21):\n",
        "    loss = train_one_epoch()\n",
        "    acc = evaluate()\n",
        "    print(f\"Epoch {epoch:02d} | Loss={loss:.4f} | Test Acc={acc*100:.2f}%\")\n"
      ],

            "\u001b[0;32m/usr/local/lib/python3.12/dist-packages/torch/nn/modules/conv.py\u001b[0m in \u001b[0;36m_conv_forward\u001b[0;34m(self, input, weight, bias)\u001b[0m\n\u001b[1;32m    364\u001b[0m                 \u001b[0mself\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m.\u001b[0m\u001b[0mgroups\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m,\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m\u001b[0m\u001b[0m\n\u001b[1;32m    365\u001b[0m             )\n\u001b[0;32m--> 366\u001b[0;31m         return F.conv1d(\n\u001b[0m\u001b[1;32m    367\u001b[0m             \u001b[0minput\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m,\u001b[0m \u001b[0mweight\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m,\u001b[0m \u001b[0mbias\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m,\u001b[0m \u001b[0mself\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m.\u001b[0m\u001b[0mstride\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m,\u001b[0m \u001b[0mself\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m.\u001b[0m\u001b[0mpadding\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m,\u001b[0m \u001b[0mself\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m.\u001b[0m\u001b[0mdilation\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m,\u001b[0m \u001b[0mself\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m.\u001b[0m\u001b[0mgroups\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m\u001b[0m\u001b[0;34m\u001b[0m\u001b[0m\n\u001b[1;32m    368\u001b[0m         )\n",

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "✅ Fixed: TCN → GCN → GoG → Classifier for PenDigits dataset\n",
        "Includes:\n",
        "- Correct Conv1d input order\n",
        "- Working DataLoader\n",
        "- Clean training loop (Colab + GPU ready)\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset\n",
        "from tqdm import tqdm\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "print(\"Device:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 1️⃣ Data Loading (PenDigits)\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "train_path = \"/content/PenDigits_TRAIN.ts\"\n",
        "test_path  = \"/content/PenDigits_TEST.ts\"\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train_df, y_train = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(train_path)\n",
        "X_test_df, y_test   = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(test_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "def to_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Convert sktime DataFrame to numpy array: (samples, timesteps, features).\"\"\"\n",
        "    out = []\n",
        "    for row in X_df.itertuples(index=False):\n",
        "        seqs = []\n",
        "        for v in row:\n",
        "            v = np.asarray(v)\n",
        "            seqs.append(v)\n",
        "        out.append(np.stack(seqs, axis=1))  # (timesteps, features)\n",
        "    return np.stack(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "X_train = to_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "X_test  = to_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(f\"✅ Loaded shapes -> X_train: {X_train.shape}, X_test: {X_test.shape}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "# Convert to tensors\n",
        "X_train = torch.tensor(X_train, dtype=torch.float32)\n",
        "y_train = torch.tensor(y_train.astype(int), dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "X_test  = torch.tensor(X_test, dtype=torch.float32)\n",
        "y_test  = torch.tensor(y_test.astype(int), dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_ds = TensorDataset(X_train, y_train)\n",
        "test_ds  = TensorDataset(X_test, y_test)\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=128, shuffle=True)\n",
        "test_loader  = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=128)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 2️⃣ Model Components\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "class TemporalConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Temporal 1D convolution block\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size=3):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.conv = nn.Conv1d(in_channels, out_channels, kernel_size, padding=1)\n",
        "        self.bn = nn.BatchNorm1d(out_channels)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: (B, F, T)\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.bn(self.conv(x)))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Basic GCN layer\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_features, out_features):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_features, out_features)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, adj):\n",
        "        # x: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        A_norm = adj + torch.eye(adj.size(-1), device=x.device)\n",
        "        D_inv_sqrt = torch.pow(A_norm.sum(dim=-1), -0.5)\n",
        "        D_inv_sqrt[torch.isinf(D_inv_sqrt)] = 0\n",
        "        D_mat = torch.diag_embed(D_inv_sqrt)\n",
        "        A_norm = torch.bmm(torch.bmm(D_mat, A_norm), D_mat)\n",
        "        out = torch.bmm(A_norm, x)\n",
        "        out = self.linear(out)\n",
        "        return F.relu(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GoG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Graph-of-Graphs Aggregation\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_features, out_features):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Linear(in_features, out_features)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, graphs):\n",
        "        # graphs: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        g_repr = graphs.mean(dim=1)  # mean pooling\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.fc(g_repr))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class TCN_GCN_GoG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, num_features=2, num_classes=10):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.tcn = TemporalConv(num_features, 64)\n",
        "        self.gcn = GraphConv(64, 64)\n",
        "        self.gog = GoG(64, 128)\n",
        "        self.classifier = nn.Linear(128, num_classes)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        # ✅ Fix: permute for Conv1d\n",
        "        x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)  # (B, F, T)\n",
        "        x = self.tcn(x)         # (B, 64, T)\n",
        "        x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)  # (B, T, 64)\n",
        "\n",
        "        B, N, _ = x.shape\n",
        "        # Fully connected adjacency for simplicity\n",
        "        adj = torch.ones(B, N, N, device=x.device)\n",
        "\n",
        "        x = self.gcn(x, adj)\n",
        "        x = self.gog(x)\n",
        "        return self.classifier(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 3️⃣ Training + Evaluation\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "model = TCN_GCN_GoG(num_features=2, num_classes=len(torch.unique(y_train))).to(device)\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=1e-3)\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "\n",
        "def train_one_epoch():\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    total_loss = 0\n",
        "    for xb, yb in tqdm(train_loader, desc=\"Train\", leave=False):\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        preds = model(xb)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(preds, yb)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "        total_loss += loss.item() * xb.size(0)\n",
        "    return total_loss / len(train_loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "def evaluate(loader):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    correct = 0\n",
        "    with torch.no_grad():\n",
        "        for xb, yb in loader:\n",
        "            xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "            preds = model(xb).argmax(dim=1)\n",
        "            correct += (preds == yb).sum().item()\n",
        "    return correct / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "# 4️⃣ Training Loop\n",
        "# ============================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, 31):\n",
        "    loss = train_one_epoch()\n",
        "    acc = evaluate(test_loader)\n",
        "    print(f\"Epoch {epoch:02d} | Loss: {loss:.4f} | Test Acc: {acc*100:.2f}%\")\n"
      ],
      "metadata": {

      "source": [
        "# Track training and test accuracy\n",
        "train_accs = []\n",
        "test_accs = []\n",
        "\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, 31):\n",
        "    loss = train_one_epoch()\n",
        "    acc_train = evaluate(train_loader)\n",
        "    acc_test = evaluate(test_loader)\n",
        "    train_accs.append(acc_train)\n",
        "    test_accs.append(acc_test)\n",
        "    print(f\"Epoch {epoch:02d} | Loss={loss:.4f} | Train={acc_train*100:.2f}% | Test={acc_test*100:.2f}%\")\n"

          },
          "metadata": {}
        }
      ]
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# Universal Multivariate Time Series Classifier\n",
        "#  TCN → GCN → GoG → Classifier\n",
        "#  Works on all UEA Multivariate datasets\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "!pip install sktime torch torchvision torchaudio tqdm --quiet\n",
        "\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset\n",
        "from tqdm import tqdm\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "print(\"Running on:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 1️⃣ Dataset Loader (Flexible for any UEA Multivariate Dataset)\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def load_uea_dataset(dataset_name, base_dir=\"/content\"):\n",
        "    # train_path = f\"{base_dir}/{dataset_name}_TRAIN.ts\"\n",
        "    # test_path  = f\"{base_dir}/{dataset_name}_TEST.ts\"\n",
        "    train_path = \"/content/PEMS-SF_TRAIN.ts\"\n",
        "    test_path  = \"/content/PEMS-SF_TEST.ts\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    X_train_df, y_train = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(train_path)\n",
        "    X_test_df, y_test   = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(test_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def to_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "        out = []\n",
        "        for row in X_df.itertuples(index=False):\n",
        "            seqs = []\n",
        "            for v in row:\n",
        "                seqs.append(np.asarray(v))\n",
        "            out.append(np.stack(seqs, axis=1))\n",
        "        return np.stack(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "    X_train = to_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "    X_test  = to_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ✅ Universal label encoding fix\n",
        "    le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "    y_train = le.fit_transform(np.array(y_train).astype(str))\n",
        "    y_test  = le.transform(np.array(y_test).astype(str))\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"✅ Loaded {dataset_name}:\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   X_train: {X_train.shape}, X_test: {X_test.shape}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Classes: {len(le.classes_)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 2️⃣ Model Components\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "class TemporalConvNet(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Stacked TCN blocks\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_c, hidden_c=128, layers=3, kernel_size=3):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        mods = []\n",
        "        for i in range(layers):\n",
        "            dilation = 2**i\n",
        "            mods.append(nn.Conv1d(in_c if i == 0 else hidden_c,\n",
        "                                  hidden_c,\n",
        "                                  kernel_size,\n",
        "                                  padding=dilation,\n",
        "                                  dilation=dilation))\n",
        "            mods.append(nn.BatchNorm1d(hidden_c))\n",
        "            mods.append(nn.ReLU())\n",
        "        self.net = nn.Sequential(*mods)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        return self.net(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Basic GCN layer\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_f, out_f):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_f, out_f)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, adj):\n",
        "        # x: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        I = torch.eye(adj.size(-1), device=x.device).unsqueeze(0)\n",
        "        A_hat = adj + I\n",
        "        D_inv_sqrt = torch.pow(A_hat.sum(-1), -0.5)\n",
        "        D_inv_sqrt[torch.isinf(D_inv_sqrt)] = 0\n",
        "        D = torch.diag_embed(D_inv_sqrt)\n",
        "        A_norm = D @ A_hat @ D\n",
        "        out = A_norm @ x\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.linear(out))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GoG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Graph-of-Graphs Aggregation\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_f, out_f):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Linear(in_f, out_f)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, graphs):\n",
        "        # graphs: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        pooled = graphs.mean(dim=1)\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.fc(pooled))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 3️⃣ Full Unified Model\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "class Universal_MTS_Model(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, num_classes, tcn_hidden=128,\n",
        "                 gcn_hidden=128, gog_hidden=256, nodes_as=\"auto\"):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        nodes_as: \"time\", \"channels\", or \"auto\"\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.nodes_as = nodes_as\n",
        "        self.tcn = TemporalConvNet(in_c=in_channels, hidden_c=tcn_hidden, layers=3)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gcn1 = GraphConv(tcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gcn2 = GraphConv(gcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gog = GoG(gcn_hidden, gog_hidden)\n",
        "        self.classifier = nn.Linear(gog_hidden, num_classes)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def build_adjacency(self, x):\n",
        "        # Cosine adjacency between nodes\n",
        "        x_norm = F.normalize(x, dim=-1)\n",
        "        adj = torch.bmm(x_norm, x_norm.transpose(1, 2))\n",
        "        return adj\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: (B, T, C)\n",
        "        B, T, C = x.shape\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Choose nodes = channels or timesteps dynamically\n",
        "        if self.nodes_as == \"auto\":\n",
        "            self.nodes_as = \"channels\" if C > T else \"time\"\n",
        "\n",
        "        if self.nodes_as == \"time\":\n",
        "            # Nodes = timesteps\n",
        "            x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)      # (B, C, T)\n",
        "            x = self.tcn(x)             # (B, hidden, T)\n",
        "            x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)      # (B, T, hidden)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            # Nodes = channels\n",
        "            x = x.transpose(1, 2)       # (B, C, T)\n",
        "            x = self.tcn(x)             # (B, hidden, T)\n",
        "            x = x.transpose(1, 2)       # (B, T, hidden)\n",
        "            # Treat T as features now\n",
        "            x = x.mean(dim=1, keepdim=True).repeat(1, C, 1)  # (B, C, hidden)\n",
        "\n",
        "        adj = self.build_adjacency(x)\n",
        "        x = self.gcn1(x, adj)\n",
        "        x = self.gcn2(x, adj)\n",
        "        x = self.gog(x)\n",
        "        return self.classifier(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 4️⃣ Training Loop\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def train(model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    total_loss = 0\n",
        "    for xb, yb in tqdm(train_loader, desc=\"Train\", leave=False):\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        out = model(xb)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(out, yb)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "        total_loss += loss.item() * xb.size(0)\n",
        "    return total_loss / len(train_loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def evaluate(model, loader):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    correct = 0\n",
        "    for xb, yb in loader:\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        preds = model(xb).argmax(1)\n",
        "        correct += (preds == yb).sum().item()\n",
        "    return correct / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 5️⃣ Run Example (on a heavy dataset)\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# 🔸 Choose dataset name (from the UEA multivariate archive)\n",
        "dataset_name = \"PEMS-SF\"   # You can also try \"NATOPS\", \"ArticulatoryWordRecognition\", etc.\n",
        "\n",
        "# ⚠️ Upload the two .ts files to /content before running this cell\n",
        "X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test = load_uea_dataset(dataset_name)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_ds = TensorDataset(torch.tensor(X_train, dtype=torch.float32),\n",
        "                         torch.tensor(y_train, dtype=torch.long))\n",
        "test_ds  = TensorDataset(torch.tensor(X_test, dtype=torch.float32),\n",
        "                         torch.tensor(y_test, dtype=torch.long))\n",
        "\n",
        "batch_size = 16 if X_train.shape[2] > 200 else 64  # handle large channel counts safely\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True)\n",
        "test_loader  = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=batch_size)\n",
        "\n",
        "num_classes = len(np.unique(y_train))\n",
        "model = Universal_MTS_Model(in_channels=X_train.shape[2],\n",
        "                            num_classes=num_classes).to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=1e-3, weight_decay=1e-4)\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "\n",
        "epochs = 100\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, epochs+1):\n",
        "    loss = train(model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion)\n",
        "    acc = evaluate(model, test_loader)\n",
        "    print(f\"Epoch {epoch:02d} | Loss={loss:.4f} | TestAcc={acc*100:.2f}%\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"✅ Training complete.\")\n"

            "✅ Training complete.\n"

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# Universal Multivariate Time Series Classifier\n",
        "#  TCN → GCN → GoG → Classifier\n",
        "#  Works on all UEA Multivariate datasets\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "!pip install sktime torch torchvision torchaudio tqdm --quiet\n",
        "\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset\n",
        "from tqdm import tqdm\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "print(\"Running on:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 1️⃣ Dataset Loader (Flexible for any UEA Multivariate Dataset)\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def load_uea_dataset(dataset_name, base_dir=\"/content\"):\n",
        "    # train_path = f\"{base_dir}/{dataset_name}_TRAIN.ts\"\n",
        "    # test_path  = f\"{base_dir}/{dataset_name}_TEST.ts\"\n",
        "    train_path = \"/content/PEMS-SF_TRAIN.ts\"\n",
        "    test_path  = \"/content/PEMS-SF_TEST.ts\"\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "    X_train_df, y_train = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(train_path)\n",
        "    X_test_df, y_test   = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(test_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def to_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "        out = []\n",
        "        for row in X_df.itertuples(index=False):\n",
        "            seqs = []\n",
        "            for v in row:\n",
        "                seqs.append(np.asarray(v))\n",
        "            out.append(np.stack(seqs, axis=1))\n",
        "        return np.stack(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "    X_train = to_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "    X_test  = to_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # ✅ Universal label encoding fix\n",
        "    le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "    y_train = le.fit_transform(np.array(y_train).astype(str))\n",
        "    y_test  = le.transform(np.array(y_test).astype(str))\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"✅ Loaded {dataset_name}:\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   X_train: {X_train.shape}, X_test: {X_test.shape}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"   Classes: {len(le.classes_)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 2️⃣ Model Components\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "class TemporalConvNet(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Stacked TCN blocks\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_c, hidden_c=128, layers=3, kernel_size=3):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        mods = []\n",
        "        for i in range(layers):\n",
        "            dilation = 2**i\n",
        "            mods.append(nn.Conv1d(in_c if i == 0 else hidden_c,\n",
        "                                  hidden_c,\n",
        "                                  kernel_size,\n",
        "                                  padding=dilation,\n",
        "                                  dilation=dilation))\n",
        "            mods.append(nn.BatchNorm1d(hidden_c))\n",
        "            mods.append(nn.ReLU())\n",
        "        self.net = nn.Sequential(*mods)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        return self.net(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Basic GCN layer\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_f, out_f):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_f, out_f)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, adj):\n",
        "        # x: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        I = torch.eye(adj.size(-1), device=x.device).unsqueeze(0)\n",
        "        A_hat = adj + I\n",
        "        D_inv_sqrt = torch.pow(A_hat.sum(-1), -0.5)\n",
        "        D_inv_sqrt[torch.isinf(D_inv_sqrt)] = 0\n",
        "        D = torch.diag_embed(D_inv_sqrt)\n",
        "        A_norm = D @ A_hat @ D\n",
        "        out = A_norm @ x\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.linear(out))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GoG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Graph-of-Graphs Aggregation\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_f, out_f):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Linear(in_f, out_f)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, graphs):\n",
        "        # graphs: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        pooled = graphs.mean(dim=1)\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.fc(pooled))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 3️⃣ Full Unified Model\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "class Enhanced_MTS_Model(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, num_classes):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        # ----- TCN -----\n",
        "        self.tcn = TemporalConvNet(in_c=in_channels, hidden_c=256, layers=5)\n",
        "        # ----- Learnable + cosine adjacency -----\n",
        "        self.W = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(256, 256))\n",
        "        # ----- GCN -----\n",
        "        self.gcn1 = GraphConv(256, 256)\n",
        "        self.gcn2 = GraphConv(256, 256)\n",
        "        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(256)\n",
        "        self.drop = nn.Dropout(0.3)\n",
        "        # ----- GoG -----\n",
        "        self.meta_gcn = GraphConv(256, 256)\n",
        "        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(\n",
        "            nn.Linear(256, 128), nn.ReLU(), nn.Dropout(0.3),\n",
        "            nn.Linear(128, num_classes)\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        B, T, C = x.shape\n",
        "        x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)           # (B, C, T)\n",
        "        x = self.tcn(x)                  # (B, 256, T)\n",
        "        x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)           # (B, T, 256)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Learnable + cosine adjacency\n",
        "        cos_adj = torch.bmm(F.normalize(x, dim=-1), F.normalize(x, dim=-1).transpose(1,2))\n",
        "        learn_adj = torch.sigmoid(self.W @ self.W.T)\n",
        "        adj = 0.5*cos_adj + 0.5*learn_adj.unsqueeze(0)\n",
        "\n",
        "        h = self.gcn1(x, adj)\n",
        "        h = self.drop(self.norm(self.gcn2(h, adj)) + h)\n",
        "        g_repr = h.mean(dim=1)           # (B, 256)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ---- meta graph across batch ----\n",
        "        meta_adj = torch.bmm(F.normalize(g_repr.unsqueeze(1), dim=-1),\n",
        "                             F.normalize(g_repr.unsqueeze(1), dim=-1).transpose(1,2)).squeeze(1)\n",
        "        g_repr = self.meta_gcn(g_repr.unsqueeze(1), meta_adj.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0)\n",
        "\n",
        "        return self.classifier(g_repr)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 4️⃣ Training Loop\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "def train(model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    total_loss = 0\n",
        "    for xb, yb in tqdm(train_loader, desc=\"Train\", leave=False):\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        out = model(xb)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(out, yb)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "        total_loss += loss.item() * xb.size(0)\n",
        "    return total_loss / len(train_loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def evaluate(model, loader):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    correct = 0\n",
        "    for xb, yb in loader:\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        preds = model(xb).argmax(1)\n",
        "        correct += (preds == yb).sum().item()\n",
        "    return correct / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 5️⃣ Run Example (on a heavy dataset)\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "# 🔸 Choose dataset name (from the UEA multivariate archive)\n",
        "dataset_name = \"PEMS-SF\"   # You can also try \"NATOPS\", \"ArticulatoryWordRecognition\", etc.\n",
        "\n",
        "# ⚠️ Upload the two .ts files to /content before running this cell\n",
        "X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test = load_uea_dataset(dataset_name)\n",
        "\n",
        "train_ds = TensorDataset(torch.tensor(X_train, dtype=torch.float32),\n",
        "                         torch.tensor(y_train, dtype=torch.long))\n",
        "test_ds  = TensorDataset(torch.tensor(X_test, dtype=torch.float32),\n",
        "                         torch.tensor(y_test, dtype=torch.long))\n",
        "\n",
        "batch_size = 16 if X_train.shape[2] > 200 else 64  # handle large channel counts safely\n",
        "train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True)\n",
        "test_loader  = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=batch_size)\n",
        "\n",
        "num_classes = len(np.unique(y_train))\n",
        "model = Universal_MTS_Model(in_channels=X_train.shape[2],\n",
        "                            num_classes=num_classes).to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=1e-3, weight_decay=1e-4)\n",
        "criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "\n",
        "epochs = 100\n",
        "for epoch in range(1, epochs+1):\n",
        "    loss = train(model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion)\n",
        "    acc = evaluate(model, test_loader)\n",
        "    print(f\"Epoch {epoch:02d} | Loss={loss:.4f} | TestAcc={acc*100:.2f}%\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"✅ Training complete.\")\n"
      ],

        },
        {
          "output_type": "stream",

            "Epoch 100 | Loss=0.0281 | TestAcc=78.03%\n",
            "✅ Training complete.\n"

      ]

    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# Universal Multivariate Time Series Classifier\n",
        "#  TCN → GCN → GoG → Classifier\n",
        "#  Works on all UEA Multivariate datasets (download by name)\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "!pip install -q sktime torch torchvision torchaudio tqdm\n",
        "\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset\n",
        "from tqdm import tqdm\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_UCR_UEA_dataset\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder\n",
        "import warnings\n",
        "import os\n",
        "\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "print(\"Running on:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 1️⃣ Dataset Loader (fetch by name from the UCR/UEA archive)\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "def load_uea_dataset(dataset_name, extract_path=None, fallback_from_local=True, local_base=\"/content\"):\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    Fetch a UEA/UCR dataset by name using sktime's loader.\n",
        "    Returns:\n",
        "        X_train: numpy array (N_train, T, C)\n",
        "        y_train: numpy array (N_train,)\n",
        "        X_test:  numpy array (N_test, T, C)\n",
        "        y_test:  numpy array (N_test,)\n",
        "    Behavior:\n",
        "      - Uses sktime.datasets.load_UCR_UEA_dataset to download & load data.\n",
        "      - Requests return_type=\"numpy3D\" which gives (instances, variables, time).\n",
        "      - Transposes to (instances, time, variables) which the model expects.\n",
        "      - If the online fetch fails and fallback_from_local is True, it will try the local\n",
        "        files <local_base>/<dataset>_TRAIN.ts and <dataset>_TEST.ts using the same loader.\n",
        "    \"\"\"\n",
        "    try:\n",
        "        # load train and test separately to keep API simple and memory predictable\n",
        "        X_train_np, y_train = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(\n",
        "            name=dataset_name, split=\"train\", return_X_y=True, return_type=\"numpy3D\",\n",
        "            extract_path=extract_path\n",
        "        )\n",
        "        X_test_np, y_test = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(\n",
        "            name=dataset_name, split=\"test\", return_X_y=True, return_type=\"numpy3D\",\n",
        "            extract_path=extract_path\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        # sktime numpy3D: (instances, variables, time_index)\n",
        "        # convert to (instances, time_index, variables)\n",
        "        X_train = np.transpose(X_train_np, (0, 2, 1))\n",
        "        X_test  = np.transpose(X_test_np,  (0, 2, 1))\n",
        "\n",
        "        # ensure labels are numpy arrays of strings initially\n",
        "        y_train = np.array(y_train).astype(str)\n",
        "        y_test  = np.array(y_test).astype(str)\n",
        "\n",
        "        # use a single LabelEncoder for consistency across train/test\n",
        "        le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "        le.fit(np.concatenate([y_train, y_test], axis=0))\n",
        "        y_train_enc = le.transform(y_train)\n",
        "        y_test_enc  = le.transform(y_test)\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(f\"✅ Downloaded and loaded dataset '{dataset_name}' via sktime:\")\n",
        "        print(f\"   X_train: {X_train.shape}, X_test: {X_test.shape}\")\n",
        "        print(f\"   Num classes: {len(le.classes_)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "        return X_train, y_train_enc, X_test, y_test_enc\n",
        "\n",
        "    except Exception as e:\n",
        "        warnings.warn(f\"Could not fetch '{dataset_name}' from remote via sktime: {e}\")\n",
        "        if fallback_from_local:\n",
        "            # Try local .ts files in local_base\n",
        "            train_path = os.path.join(local_base, f\"{dataset_name}_TRAIN.ts\")\n",
        "            test_path  = os.path.join(local_base, f\"{dataset_name}_TEST.ts\")\n",
        "            try:\n",
        "                from sktime.datasets import load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe\n",
        "                print(\"Attempting to load from local .ts files:\", train_path, test_path)\n",
        "                X_train_df, y_train = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(train_path)\n",
        "                X_test_df, y_test   = load_from_tsfile_to_dataframe(test_path)\n",
        "\n",
        "                def to_numpy(X_df):\n",
        "                    out = []\n",
        "                    for row in X_df.itertuples(index=False):\n",
        "                        seqs = []\n",
        "                        for v in row:\n",
        "                            seqs.append(np.asarray(v))\n",
        "                        out.append(np.stack(seqs, axis=1))\n",
        "                    return np.stack(out)\n",
        "\n",
        "                X_train = to_numpy(X_train_df)\n",
        "                X_test  = to_numpy(X_test_df)\n",
        "\n",
        "                le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "                le.fit(np.concatenate([np.array(y_train).astype(str), np.array(y_test).astype(str)], axis=0))\n",
        "                y_train_enc = le.transform(np.array(y_train).astype(str))\n",
        "                y_test_enc  = le.transform(np.array(y_test).astype(str))\n",
        "\n",
        "                print(f\"✅ Loaded '{dataset_name}' from local files.\")\n",
        "                print(f\"   X_train: {X_train.shape}, X_test: {X_test.shape}\")\n",
        "                print(f\"   Num classes: {len(le.classes_)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "                return X_train, y_train_enc, X_test, y_test_enc\n",
        "\n",
        "            except Exception as e2:\n",
        "                raise RuntimeError(f\"Failed to load dataset from remote and local fallback failed: {e2}\") from e2\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            raise\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 2️⃣ Model Components (unchanged)\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "class TemporalConvNet(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Stacked TCN blocks\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_c, hidden_c=128, layers=3, kernel_size=3):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        mods = []\n",
        "        for i in range(layers):\n",
        "            dilation = 2**i\n",
        "            mods.append(nn.Conv1d(in_c if i == 0 else hidden_c,\n",
        "                                  hidden_c,\n",
        "                                  kernel_size,\n",
        "                                  padding=dilation,\n",
        "                                  dilation=dilation))\n",
        "            mods.append(nn.BatchNorm1d(hidden_c))\n",
        "            mods.append(nn.ReLU())\n",
        "        self.net = nn.Sequential(*mods)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        return self.net(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Basic GCN layer\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_f, out_f):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.linear = nn.Linear(in_f, out_f)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, adj):\n",
        "        # x: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        I = torch.eye(adj.size(-1), device=x.device).unsqueeze(0)\n",
        "        A_hat = adj + I\n",
        "        D_inv_sqrt = torch.pow(A_hat.sum(-1), -0.5)\n",
        "        D_inv_sqrt[torch.isinf(D_inv_sqrt)] = 0\n",
        "        D = torch.diag_embed(D_inv_sqrt)\n",
        "        A_norm = D @ A_hat @ D\n",
        "        out = A_norm @ x\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.linear(out))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "class GoG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    \"\"\"Graph-of-Graphs Aggregation\"\"\"\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_f, out_f):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Linear(in_f, out_f)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, graphs):\n",
        "        # graphs: (B, N, F)\n",
        "        pooled = graphs.mean(dim=1)\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.fc(pooled))\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 3️⃣ Full Unified Model\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "class Universal_MTS_Model(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, num_classes, tcn_hidden=128,\n",
        "                 gcn_hidden=128, gog_hidden=256, nodes_as=\"auto\"):\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        nodes_as: \"time\", \"channels\", or \"auto\"\n",
        "        \"\"\"\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.nodes_as = nodes_as\n",
        "        self.tcn = TemporalConvNet(in_c=in_channels, hidden_c=tcn_hidden, layers=3)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gcn1 = GraphConv(tcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gcn2 = GraphConv(gcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.gog = GoG(gcn_hidden, gog_hidden)\n",
        "        self.classifier = nn.Linear(gog_hidden, num_classes)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def build_adjacency(self, x):\n",
        "        # Cosine adjacency between nodes\n",
        "        x_norm = F.normalize(x, dim=-1)\n",
        "        adj = torch.bmm(x_norm, x_norm.transpose(1, 2))\n",
        "        return adj\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        # x: (B, T, C)\n",
        "        B, T, C = x.shape\n",
        "\n",
        "        # Choose nodes = channels or timesteps dynamically\n",
        "        if self.nodes_as == \"auto\":\n",
        "            nodes_setting = \"channels\" if C > T else \"time\"\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            nodes_setting = self.nodes_as\n",
        "\n",
        "        if nodes_setting == \"time\":\n",
        "            # Nodes = timesteps\n",
        "            x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)      # (B, C, T)\n",
        "            x = self.tcn(x)             # (B, hidden, T)\n",
        "            x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)      # (B, T, hidden)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            # Nodes = channels\n",
        "            x = x.transpose(1, 2)       # (B, C, T)\n",
        "            x = self.tcn(x)             # (B, hidden, T)\n",
        "            x = x.transpose(1, 2)       # (B, T, hidden)\n",
        "            # Treat T as features now: pool across timesteps to get per-channel features\n",
        "            x = x.mean(dim=1, keepdim=True).repeat(1, C, 1)  # (B, C, hidden)\n",
        "\n",
        "        adj = self.build_adjacency(x)\n",
        "        x = self.gcn1(x, adj)\n",
        "        x = self.gcn2(x, adj)\n",
        "        x = self.gog(x)\n",
        "        return self.classifier(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 4️⃣ Training Loop (unchanged)\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "def train(model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    total_loss = 0\n",
        "    for xb, yb in tqdm(train_loader, desc=\"Train\", leave=False):\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        out = model(xb)\n",
        "        loss = criterion(out, yb)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "        total_loss += loss.item() * xb.size(0)\n",
        "    return total_loss / len(train_loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def evaluate(model, loader):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    correct = 0\n",
        "    for xb, yb in loader:\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        preds = model(xb).argmax(1)\n",
        "        correct += (preds == yb).sum().item()\n",
        "    return correct / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# 5️⃣ Run Example (fetches dataset by name)\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n",
        "    # 🔸 Choose dataset name (from the UEA multivariate archive)\n",
        "    dataset_name = \"UWaveGestureLibrary\"   # e.g.  \"FaceDetection\" \"PEMS-SF\", \"NATOPS\", \"ArticulatoryWordRecognition\", etc.\n",
        "\n",
        "    # this will try remote fetch; if that fails, it attempts local fallback files in /content\n",
        "    X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test = load_uea_dataset(dataset_name, fallback_from_local=True, local_base=\"/content\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    train_ds = TensorDataset(torch.tensor(X_train, dtype=torch.float32),\n",
        "                             torch.tensor(y_train, dtype=torch.long))\n",
        "    test_ds  = TensorDataset(torch.tensor(X_test, dtype=torch.float32),\n",
        "                             torch.tensor(y_test, dtype=torch.long))\n",
        "\n",
        "    batch_size = 16 if X_train.shape[2] > 200 else 64  # X_train shape: (N, T, C) => channels at axis=2\n",
        "    train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True)\n",
        "    test_loader  = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=batch_size)\n",
        "\n",
        "    num_classes = len(np.unique(y_train))\n",
        "    model = Universal_MTS_Model(in_channels=X_train.shape[2],\n",
        "                                num_classes=num_classes).to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=1e-3, weight_decay=1e-4)\n",
        "    criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n",
        "\n",
        "    epochs = 100\n",
        "    for epoch in range(1, epochs+1):\n",
        "        loss = train(model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion)\n",
        "        acc = evaluate(model, test_loader)\n",
        "        print(f\"Epoch {epoch:02d} | Loss={loss:.4f} | TestAcc={acc*100:.2f}%\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"✅ Training complete.\")\n"
      ],
