    {
      "cell_type": "markdown",
      "source": [
        "**New Updated code**"
      ],
      "metadata": {
        "id": "uQThsvJi86GC"
      }
    },
    {
      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# Universal Multivariate Time Series Classifier (IMPROVED)\n",
        "# TCN → Sparse GCN → GoG → Classifier\n",
        "# - Regularization everywhere\n",
        "# - Sparse adjacency\n",
        "# - Adaptive capacity\n",
        "# - Label smoothing\n",
        "# - Light data augmentation\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "\n",
        "!pip install -q sktime torch torchvision torchaudio tqdm\n",
        "\n",
        "import os, warnings\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "import torch\n",
        "import torch.nn as nn\n",
        "import torch.nn.functional as F\n",
        "from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset\n",
        "from tqdm import tqdm\n",
        "from sktime.datasets import load_UCR_UEA_dataset\n",
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# Device\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "device = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n",
        "print(\"Running on:\", device)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# Dataset Loader\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "def load_uea_dataset(dataset_name, extract_path=None):\n",
        "    X_train_np, y_train = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(\n",
        "        name=dataset_name, split=\"train\", return_X_y=True, return_type=\"numpy3D\",\n",
        "        extract_path=extract_path\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    X_test_np, y_test = load_UCR_UEA_dataset(\n",
        "        name=dataset_name, split=\"test\", return_X_y=True, return_type=\"numpy3D\",\n",
        "        extract_path=extract_path\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    # (N, C, T) → (N, T, C)\n",
        "    X_train = np.transpose(X_train_np, (0, 2, 1))\n",
        "    X_test  = np.transpose(X_test_np,  (0, 2, 1))\n",
        "\n",
        "    le = LabelEncoder()\n",
        "    le.fit(np.concatenate([y_train, y_test]).astype(str))\n",
        "    y_train = le.transform(np.array(y_train).astype(str))\n",
        "    y_test  = le.transform(np.array(y_test).astype(str))\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(f\"Dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Train: {X_train.shape}, Test: {X_test.shape}\")\n",
        "    print(f\"Classes: {len(le.classes_)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    return X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# Data Augmentation (light & safe)\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "def augment(x):\n",
        "    if torch.rand(1, device=x.device) < 0.3:\n",
        "        x = x + 0.01 * torch.randn_like(x)\n",
        "    if torch.rand(1, device=x.device) < 0.3:\n",
        "        scale = torch.empty(1, device=x.device).uniform_(0.9, 1.1)\n",
        "        x = x * scale\n",
        "    return x\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# Model Components\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "class TemporalConvNet(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_c, hidden_c, layers=3, dropout=0.2):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        mods = []\n",
        "        for i in range(layers):\n",
        "            dilation = 2 ** i\n",
        "            mods += [\n",
        "                nn.Conv1d(in_c if i == 0 else hidden_c,\n",
        "                          hidden_c,\n",
        "                          kernel_size=3,\n",
        "                          padding=dilation,\n",
        "                          dilation=dilation),\n",
        "                nn.BatchNorm1d(hidden_c),\n",
        "                nn.ReLU(),\n",
        "                nn.Dropout(dropout)\n",
        "            ]\n",
        "        self.net = nn.Sequential(*mods)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        return self.net(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "class GraphConv(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_f, out_f, dropout=0.3):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Linear(in_f, out_f)\n",
        "        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x, adj):\n",
        "        I = torch.eye(adj.size(-1), device=x.device).unsqueeze(0)\n",
        "        A = adj + I\n",
        "        D = torch.pow(A.sum(-1), -0.5)\n",
        "        D[torch.isinf(D)] = 0\n",
        "        D = torch.diag_embed(D)\n",
        "        A = D @ A @ D\n",
        "        x = A @ x\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.dropout(self.fc(x)))\n",
        "\n",
        "class GoG(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_f, out_f):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.fc = nn.Linear(in_f, out_f)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        return F.relu(self.fc(x.mean(dim=1)))\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# Full Model\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "class UniversalMTS(nn.Module):\n",
        "    def __init__(self, in_channels, num_classes,\n",
        "                 tcn_hidden, gcn_hidden, gog_hidden,\n",
        "                 nodes_as=\"auto\", topk=8):\n",
        "        super().__init__()\n",
        "        self.nodes_as = nodes_as\n",
        "        self.topk = topk\n",
        "\n",
        "        self.tcn = TemporalConvNet(in_channels, tcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gcn1 = GraphConv(tcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gcn2 = GraphConv(gcn_hidden, gcn_hidden)\n",
        "        self.gog = GoG(gcn_hidden, gog_hidden)\n",
        "        self.cls = nn.Linear(gog_hidden, num_classes)\n",
        "\n",
        "    def build_adj(self, x):\n",
        "        x = F.normalize(x, dim=-1)\n",
        "        sim = torch.bmm(x, x.transpose(1, 2))\n",
        "        k = min(self.topk, sim.size(-1))\n",
        "        vals, idx = torch.topk(sim, k, dim=-1)\n",
        "        mask = torch.zeros_like(sim)\n",
        "        mask.scatter_(-1, idx, 1.0)\n",
        "        return sim * mask\n",
        "\n",
        "    def forward(self, x):\n",
        "        B, T, C = x.shape\n",
        "\n",
        "        if self.nodes_as == \"auto\":\n",
        "            nodes = \"channels\" if C > T else \"time\"\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            nodes = self.nodes_as\n",
        "\n",
        "        if nodes == \"time\":\n",
        "            x = self.tcn(x.permute(0, 2, 1)).permute(0, 2, 1)\n",
        "        else:\n",
        "            x = self.tcn(x.transpose(1, 2)).transpose(1, 2)\n",
        "            x = x.mean(dim=1, keepdim=True).repeat(1, C, 1)\n",
        "\n",
        "        adj = self.build_adj(x)\n",
        "        x = self.gcn1(x, adj)\n",
        "        x = self.gcn2(x, adj)\n",
        "        x = self.gog(x)\n",
        "        return self.cls(x)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# Training & Evaluation\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "def train_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, criterion):\n",
        "    model.train()\n",
        "    total = 0\n",
        "    for xb, yb in loader:\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        xb = augment(xb)\n",
        "\n",
        "        optimizer.zero_grad()\n",
        "        loss = criterion(model(xb), yb)\n",
        "        loss.backward()\n",
        "        optimizer.step()\n",
        "        total += loss.item() * xb.size(0)\n",
        "    return total / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "@torch.no_grad()\n",
        "def evaluate(model, loader):\n",
        "    model.eval()\n",
        "    correct = 0\n",
        "    for xb, yb in loader:\n",
        "        xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n",
        "        pred = model(xb).argmax(1)\n",
        "        correct += (pred == yb).sum().item()\n",
        "    return correct / len(loader.dataset)\n",
        "\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "# Run\n",
        "# ================================================================\n",
        "if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n",
        "\n",
        "    DATASET = \"FaceDetection\"   # change freely\n",
        "\n",
        "    X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test = load_uea_dataset(DATASET)\n",
        "\n",
        "    train_ds = TensorDataset(\n",
        "        torch.tensor(X_train, dtype=torch.float32),\n",
        "        torch.tensor(y_train, dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    test_ds = TensorDataset(\n",
        "        torch.tensor(X_test, dtype=torch.float32),\n",
        "        torch.tensor(y_test, dtype=torch.long)\n",
        "    )\n",
        "\n",
        "    batch_size = 16 if X_train.shape[2] > 200 else 64\n",
        "    train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=True)\n",
        "    test_loader  = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=batch_size)\n",
        "\n",
        "    # Adaptive capacity (VERY IMPORTANT)\n",
        "    scale = 0.5 if X_train.shape[0] < 500 else 1.0\n",
        "\n",
        "    model = UniversalMTS(\n",
        "        in_channels=X_train.shape[2],\n",
        "        num_classes=len(np.unique(y_train)),\n",
        "        tcn_hidden=int(128 * scale),\n",
        "        gcn_hidden=int(128 * scale),\n",
        "        gog_hidden=int(256 * scale)\n",
        "    ).to(device)\n",
        "\n",
        "    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=1e-3, weight_decay=1e-4)\n",
        "    criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=0.1)\n",
        "\n",
        "    best = 0\n",
        "    for epoch in range(1, 101):\n",
        "        loss = train_epoch(model, train_loader, optimizer, criterion)\n",
        "        acc = evaluate(model, test_loader)\n",
        "        best = max(best, acc)\n",
        "        print(f\"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Loss {loss:.4f} | Acc {acc*100:.2f}% | Best {best*100:.2f}%\")\n",
        "\n",
        "    print(\"✅ Training complete.\")\n"
      ],
